- **dialektike**: The art of conducting the exchange — how to keep a dialogue between intelligences clear, productive, and unwasted. Dialektikē acts on the container (the exchange), never on the content. Seven gestures grouped by what they act on: **frame** (the ground), then **clarify**, **split**, **distill**, **followup** under one shared reflex — do not produce on faulty ground — then **thought** (by offering) and **consolidate** (the fruit): an unconsolidated discussion is a lost discussion. Across all of it, rhythm: the three gestures that require stepping back answer to nothing textual, so their signal… ([ChristianLemer/tekton](https://github.com/ChristianLemer/tekton/tree/HEAD/chiron/skills/dialektike))
